   | V2      | ToDoList          | DataSheet V2 + Guidance V1 +
  DetailedProcedure V1 |
   | V2      | Guidance          | DataSheet V2 + ToDoList V2 +
  DetailedProcedure V1 |
   | V2      | DetailedProcedure | DataSheet V2 + ToDoList V2 + Guidance V2
  |
   | V3      | DataSheet         | DetailedProcedure V2 + Guidance V2 + ToDoList
  V2  |
   | V3      | ToDoList          | DataSheet V3 + Guidance V2 +
  DetailedProcedure V2 |
   | V3      | Guidance          | DataSheet V3 + ToDoList V3 +
  DetailedProcedure V2 |
   | V3      | DetailedProcedure | DataSheet V3 + ToDoList V3 + Guidance V3
  |

   1 Pass 2 execution prompt: Output ProblemDefinitionPackage as strict JSON (no
  prose).

   Document content requirements:
   - DataSheet: Input/output specs (YAML), validation rules table, state
  definitions table
   - ToDoList: Checklist with - [ ] items, phase headers, Validation: hooks
   - Guidance: Principles, anti-patterns table, quality criteria list
   - DetailedProcedure: State machine spec, phase procedures with pseudocode

   Phase 3: Steps 2-3 Prompts

   Implement prompts for Steps 2 and 3.

   Step 2 Key Differences (from reference session):

   1. Input Validation Gate (in Reception phase):
   VALIDATE:
     - Step 1 state = COMPLETE
     - Human approval present
     - All required fields present

   2. Derivation Patterns Table (in Data Sheet):
   | Source Type | Derivation Pattern | Category | Priority |
   |-------------|-------------------|----------|----------|
   | Core Problem | Problem → Capability → FR | FR | must |
   | Stakeholder Need | Need → What satisfies it | FR/NFR | must/should |
   | Stakeholder Concern | Concern → What mitigates it | NFR | should |
   | Hard Constraint | Constraint → CR directly | CR | must |
   | Soft Constraint | Constraint → FR/NFR | FR/NFR | should/could |
   | Success Criterion | Criterion → Verification need | FR/NFR | must/should |

   3. Coverage Mapping (in Output):
   coverage:
     stakeholders: mapping of SH-XXX → [FR-001, FR-002, ...]
     constraints: mapping of HC-XXX → [CR-001, ...]
     problem: [requirement_ids addressing core problem]

   4. State Machine Differs:
   - RECEIVED → ANALYZING → DERIVING → VALIDATING → REVIEWING → COMPLETE
   - (DERIVING state is unique to Step 2)

   5. ID Scheme: FR-NNN, NFR-NNN, CR-NNN with source traceability to SH-, HC-,
  SC-*

   Step 3 (Objectives):
   - Input: Step 2 Requirements Package (validated, approved)
   - Output: Objectives (CAP/QUAL/COMP/INTF) with traceability to Step 2
   - Key IDs: CAP-NNN, QUAL-NNN, COMP-NNN, INTF-NNN
   - Transformation: FR→CAP, NFR→QUAL, CR→COMP
   - Success framework: minimum viable / target / aspirational tiers
   - Consolidation: Multiple requirements may map to one objective (preserve all
  source IDs)

   Phase 4: Node Wiring (nodes.py)

   Implement generate_step_output():
   async def generate_step_output(state: WorkflowState) -> dict:
       """Generate step output using LLM."""
       from orchestration.prompts import get_prompt, get_execution_prompt
       from orchestration.prompts.common import build_context, parse_json_output
       from infrastructure.llm import get_default_adapter

       adapter = get_default_adapter()

       if state.current_pass == PassType.DEFINITION:
           # Pass 1: Generate V1→V2→V3 methodology docs
           return await _generate_methodology(state, adapter)
       else:
           # Pass 2: Execute methodology to produce package
           return await _execute_methodology(state, adapter)

   async def _generate_methodology(state: WorkflowState, adapter: LLMAdapter) ->
  dict:
       """Run 12 prompts in V1→V2→V3 sequence."""
       results = {"v1": {}, "v2": {}, "v3": {}}
       doc_order = ["data_sheet", "todo_list", "guidance", "detailed_procedure"]

       for version in ["v1", "v2", "v3"]:
           prior_version = results.get(_prev_version(version), {})
           for doc_type in doc_order:
               prompt = get_prompt(state.current_step, version, doc_type)
               context = build_context(state, prior_version, results[version])
               messages = [
                   {"role": "system", "content":
  prompt["system"].format(**context)},
                   {"role": "user", "content":
  prompt["user"].format(**context)},
               ]
               response = await adapter.generate(messages)
               results[version][doc_type] = response.content
       return results

   async def _execute_methodology(state: WorkflowState, adapter: LLMAdapter) ->
  dict:
       """Execute V3 methodology to produce step package as JSON."""
       prompt = get_execution_prompt(state.current_step)
       context = build_context(state, {}, {})
       messages = [
           {"role": "system", "content": prompt["system"].format(**context)},
           {"role": "user", "content": prompt["user"].format(**context)},
       ]
       response = await adapter.generate(messages)
       return parse_json_output(response.content)

   Implement check_sufficiency():
   async def check_sufficiency(state: WorkflowState) -> bool:
       """Check if input needs clarification (Pass 2 Step 1 only)."""
       # Pass 1 is autonomous - no clarification
       if state.current_pass == PassType.DEFINITION:
           return False
       # Steps 2-3 have prior step output - no clarification
       if state.current_step != StepName.PROBLEM_DEFINITION:
           return False
       # Step 1 Pass 2: check if problem statement is sufficient
       # Use simple prompt returning JSON: {needs_clarification: bool,
  questions: [...]}
       ...

   validate_output() stays minimal:
   async def validate_output(step: StepName, output: dict) -> ValidationResult:
       """Basic structural validation only. Full schema validation deferred to
  P5.3."""
       # Check required top-level keys exist
       # Return ValidationResult(passed=True/False)

   Key Decisions

   | Decision              | Choice
  | Rationale
                                     |
   
  |-----------------------|-----------------------------------------------------
  -|------------------------
   ----------------------------------|
   | Prompt storage        | Constants in Python files
  | Version with code, easy
    to modify                        |
   | Methodology structure | {v1: {data_sheet: "...", ...}, v2: {...}, v3:
  {...}} | Matches Doc 1 iteration
    pattern                          |
   | Pass 2 output         | Strict JSON via prompt engineering
  | No llm.py changes; use
   clear instructions                |
   | Sufficiency check     | Only Pass 2 Step 1
  | Pass 1 autonomous,
   Steps 2-3 have prior output           |
   | Validation            | Basic key checks only
  | Full schema validation
   deferred to P5.3                  |
   | IR category           | Exclude at Instance 0
  | Reference session uses
   FR/NFR/CR only; IR is Instance 1+ |

   Design Questions Resolved

   | Question                                               | Answer
         |
   
  |--------------------------------------------------------|--------------------
  --------------------------
   ------|
   | Should LLM generate package_id/workflow_id/created_at? | No - inject in
  P5.3 persistence layer
         |
   | Should Step 2 include IR category?                     | No - Instance 0
  uses FR/NFR/CR only (per
   exemplar) |

   State Flow (P5.2 output only - no persistence)

   Pass 1 (DEFINITION):
     generate_step_output() returns:
       {"v1": {"data_sheet": "...", "todo_list": "...", ...}, "v2": {...}, "v3":
  {...}}
     → stored in state.methodology[step_key] by existing advance_node()

   Pass 2 (EXECUTION):
     generate_step_output() returns:
       {"stakeholders": [...], "constraints": {...}, ...}  # JSON dict
     → stored in state.artifacts[step_key] by existing advance_node()

   Note: DB persistence of these dicts is handled by P5.3, not P5.2.

  print(get_prompt('problem_definition', 'v1', 
   'data_sheet'))"

   Gate A verification (tools/verify_methodology.py) is P6.1 scope.

   Risks & Mitigations

   | Risk                        | Mitigation
  |
   
  |-----------------------------|-----------------------------------------------
  -------------|
   | LLM output quality          | Explicit structure in prompts, use exemplar
  format         |
   | JSON parse failures         | Strip markdown fences, clear error messages,
  retry prompt  |
   | Token limits for 12 prompts | Each prompt is independent; can batch or
  parallelize later |
   | Context too large for V3    | Summarize or truncate prior docs if needed
  |

   Negative Patterns to Avoid

   - Do NOT add Pydantic models to apps/api/domain/ (keep pure)
   - Do NOT modify apps/api/infrastructure/llm.py (P5.1 stable)
   - Do NOT implement artifact persistence (P5.3)
   - Do NOT create Gate A tooling (P6.1)
   - Do NOT allow prose in Pass 2 outputs (JSON only)

   Constraints Honored

   - Doc 1 methodology (V1→V2→V3, 4 docs per step)
   - Pass 1 autonomous (gate policy controls interrupts)
   - Pass 2 human-gated
   - Flat layout (apps/api/)
   - Use LLM adapter from P5.1 (unchanged)
   - Traceability IDs (SH-, FR-, CAP-*)
   - Scope-tight to P5.2 only

       Step 1 Data Sheet - Output Specification (Doc 3 §5.1)

       ProblemDefinitionPackage:
         title: string

           interpretation_notes: [string]

         stakeholders:

             name_or_group: string
             role: string
             needs: [string]
             concerns: [string]
             impact: string
         constraints:

               statement: string
               rationale: string
           soft:
             - id: string                # SC-001, SC-002, ...
               statement: string
               rationale: string
         scope:
           in:
             - id: string                # IN-001, ...
               item: string
           out:
             - id: string                # OUT-001, ...
               item: string
               rationale: string
         success_criteria:
           - id: string                  # CRT-001, ...
             metric_or_signal: string
             target: string
             how_verified: string
         assumptions:
           - id: string                  # ASM-001, ...
             statement: string
             rationale: string
             risk_if_wrong: string
         open_questions:
           required_to_proceed: [...]
           nice_to_have: [string]
         integration_points:
           - id: string                  # IP-001, ...
             description: string
             components: [string]

           requirement_themes: [string]
           nfr_watchouts: [string]
           dependencies_to_capture: [string]

       Step 2 Data Sheet - Output Specification (Doc 3 §5.2)

       RequirementsPackage:
         overview:
           summary: string
           boundaries: [string]
           total_requirements: integer
           priority_distribution: {must: int, should: int, could: int}
         requirements:
           - id: string                  # FR-001, NFR-001, CR-001, IR-001
             category: FR | NFR | CR | IR  # IR optional (empty list if none)
             priority: must | should | could
             statement: string
             rationale: string
             source: {type: string, id: string, aspect: string}
             component: [string]
             testability: {method: string, description: string}
             trace: {stakeholders: [], constraints: [], scope_in: [], ...}
         coverage_map: [...]
         conflicts_tradeoffs: [...]
         open_questions: {required_to_finalize: [], deferred: []}
         step3_handoff: {objective_mapping_rules: [], objective_seeds: {...}}

       ObjectivesPackage:
         overview:
           intent: string
           measurement_principles: [string]
           boundaries: [string]
           total_objectives: integer
           consolidation_ratio: float

             tier: primary | secondary | tertiary

             rationale: string

  str, threshold: str}

  bool}]

       Data Sheet V3 General Format

       Considering: Detailed Procedure V2 + Guidance Document V2 + To Do List V2

       ### Input Specification
       | Field | Type | Required | Description |
       |-------|------|----------|-------------|
       | [step-specific inputs] | ... | ... | ... |

       |---------|-------|------|----------|
       | V1 | ... | ... | Error/Warning |

       ### State Definitions
       | State | Entry Condition | Valid Transitions |
       |-------|-----------------|-------------------|
       | RECEIVED | Input logged | → ANALYZING |
       | ... | ... | ... |

       To Do List V3 Format

       Considering: Data Sheet V3 + Guidance Document V2 + Detailed Procedure V2

       #### 1. Reception [RECEIVED → ANALYZING]
       - [ ] Log raw_problem_description with timestamp
       - [ ] Initialize output structure with metadata
       - [ ] Set state: ANALYZING
       - **Validation:** Input logged, structure initialized

       #### 2. Stakeholder Analysis [STRUCTURING]
       - [ ] For each stakeholder (3-6):
         - [ ] Assign id (SH-XXX)
         - [ ] Define name, role
         - [ ] Mark source (quoted|paraphrased|inferred)
       - **Validation:** V2 (count), V3 (inference basis)

       Guidance Document V3 Format

       Considering: Data Sheet V3 + To Do List V3 + Detailed Procedure V2

       ### Purpose
       [Orient the agent to context and principles]

       ### Context
       **Position in Workflow:** Step 1 is the foundation...

       ### Core Principles
       #### 1. Stated vs. Actual Problem
       - **Stated Problem:** What the user literally said
       - **Core Problem:** What actually needs to be solved

       ### Anti-Patterns
       | Anti-Pattern | Symptom | Remedy |
       |--------------|---------|--------|
       | Conflating stated/actual | No difference_explanation | Always check |

       ### Quality Criteria for Step 1 Output
       1. Core problem is singular, focused, ≤ 2 sentences
       2. 3-6 stakeholders with complete attributes

       Detailed Procedure V3 Format

       Considering: Data Sheet V3 + To Do List V3 + Guidance Document V3

       ### State Machine Specification
       STATES = {RECEIVED, ANALYZING, ELICITING, STRUCTURING, VALIDATING,
  REVIEWING, COMPLETE}
       TRANSITIONS:
         RECEIVED    → ANALYZING   : on begin_processing()
         ANALYZING   → ELICITING   : on critical_gaps_exist()
         ANALYZING   → STRUCTURING : on no_critical_gaps()

       ### Phase 1: Reception
       **State:** RECEIVED
       **Input:** raw_problem_description
       **Procedure:**
       1. LOG input with timestamp
       2. INITIALIZE Step1Output structure
       3. TRANSITION to ANALYZING
       **Validation Gate:** Input logged, structure initialized

       def build_context(state: WorkflowState, prior_docs: dict, current_docs:
  dict) -> dict:
           """Build context dict for prompt formatting."""
           return {
               "instance_number": state.instance_number,
               "step_name": state.current_step.value,
               "original_problem": state.original_problem,
               "prior_step_output":
  state.artifacts.get(prev_step(state.current_step)),
               "prior_version_docs": prior_docs,
               "current_version_docs": current_docs,
               "v3_methodology": state.methodology.get(state.current_step.value,
  {}).get("v3"),
               "human_feedback": state.step_state.human_feedback,
           }

       | Core Problem | Problem → Capability → FR | FR | must |
       | Stakeholder Need | Need → What satisfies it | FR/NFR | must/should |
       | Stakeholder Concern | Concern → What mitigates it | NFR | should |
       | Hard Constraint | Constraint → CR directly | CR | must |
       | Soft Constraint | Constraint → FR/NFR | FR/NFR | should/could |
       | Success Criterion | Criterion → Verification need | FR/NFR |
  must/should |

       3. Coverage Mapping (in Output):
       coverage:
         stakeholders: mapping of SH-XXX → [FR-001, FR-002, ...]
         constraints: mapping of HC-XXX → [CR-001, ...]
         problem: [requirement_ids addressing core problem]

       4. State Machine Differs:
       - RECEIVED → ANALYZING → DERIVING → VALIDATING → REVIEWING → COMPLETE
       - (DERIVING state is unique to Step 2)

       5. ID Scheme: FR-NNN, NFR-NNN, CR-NNN, IR-NNN with source traceability to
  SH-, HC-, SC-, IP-
         - IR (Interface Requirements) is optional - empty list if no
  integration_points drive 
       requirements

       Step 3 (Objectives):
       - Input: Step 2 Requirements Package (validated, approved)
       - Output: Objectives (CAP/QUAL/COMP/INTF) with traceability to Step 2
       - Key IDs: CAP-NNN, QUAL-NNN, COMP-NNN, INTF-NNN
       - Transformation: FR→CAP, NFR→QUAL, CR→COMP, IR→INTF
       - INTF is optional - empty list if no IR requirements in Step 2
       - Success framework: minimum viable / target / aspirational tiers
       - Consolidation: Multiple requirements may map to one objective (preserve
  all source IDs)

       Phase 4: Node Wiring (nodes.py)

       Implement generate_step_output():
       async def generate_step_output(state: WorkflowState) -> dict:
           """Generate step output using LLM."""
           from orchestration.prompts import get_prompt, get_execution_prompt
           from orchestration.prompts.common import build_context,
  parse_json_output
           from infrastructure.llm import get_default_adapter

           adapter = get_default_adapter()

           if state.current_pass == PassType.DEFINITION:
               # Pass 1: Generate V1→V2→V3 methodology docs
               return await _generate_methodology(state, adapter)
           else:
               # Pass 2: Execute methodology to produce package
               return await _execute_methodology(state, adapter)

       async def _generate_methodology(state: WorkflowState, adapter:
  LLMAdapter) -> dict:
           """Run 12 prompts in V1→V2→V3 sequence."""
           results = {"v1": {}, "v2": {}, "v3": {}}
           doc_order = ["data_sheet", "todo_list", "guidance",
  "detailed_procedure"]

           for version in ["v1", "v2", "v3"]:
               prior_version = results.get(_prev_version(version), {})
               for doc_type in doc_order:
                   prompt = get_prompt(state.current_step, version, doc_type)
                   context = build_context(state, prior_version,
  results[version])
                   messages = [
                       {"role": "system", "content":
  prompt["system"].format(**context)},
                       {"role": "user", "content":
  prompt["user"].format(**context)},
                   ]
                   response = await adapter.generate(messages)
                   results[version][doc_type] = response.content
           return results

       async def _execute_methodology(state: WorkflowState, adapter: LLMAdapter)
  -> dict:
           """Execute V3 methodology to produce step package as JSON."""
           prompt = get_execution_prompt(state.current_step)
           context = build_context(state, {}, {})
           messages = [
               {"role": "system", "content":
  prompt["system"].format(**context)},
               {"role": "user", "content": prompt["user"].format(**context)},
           ]
           response = await adapter.generate(messages)
           return parse_json_output(response.content)

       Implement check_sufficiency():
       async def check_sufficiency(state: WorkflowState) -> bool:
           """Check if input needs clarification (Pass 2 Step 1 only)."""
           # Pass 1 is autonomous - no clarification
           if state.current_pass == PassType.DEFINITION:
               return False
           # Steps 2-3 have prior step output - no clarification
           if state.current_step != StepName.PROBLEM_DEFINITION:
               return False
           # Step 1 Pass 2: check if problem statement is sufficient
           # Use simple prompt returning JSON: {needs_clarification: bool,
  questions: [...]}
           ...

       validate_output() stays minimal:
       async def validate_output(step: StepName, output: dict) ->
  ValidationResult:
           """Basic structural validation only. Full schema validation deferred
  to P5.3."""
           # Check required top-level keys exist
           # Return ValidationResult(passed=True/False)

       Key Decisions

       | Decision              | Choice
  | Rationale       
                                                        |
       
  |-----------------------|-----------------------------------------------------
  -|-----------------
       -------------------------------------------------|
       | Prompt storage        | Constants in Python files
  | Version with 
       code, easy to modify                                |
       | Methodology structure | {v1: {data_sheet: "...", ...}, v2: {...}, v3:
  {...}} | Matches Doc 1 
       iteration pattern                                  |
       | Pass 2 output         | Strict JSON via prompt engineering
  | No llm.py 
       changes; use clear instructions                        |
       | Sufficiency check     | Only Pass 2 Step 1
  | Pass 1 
       autonomous, Steps 2-3 have prior output                   |
       | Validation            | Basic key checks only
  | Full schema 
       validation deferred to P5.3                          |
       | IR/INTF categories    | Allow as optional (empty list if none)
  | Per addendum - 
       don't forbid categories in prompts                |
       | Doc 3 package names   | Use exact Doc 3 field names
  | 
       ProblemDefinitionPackage, RequirementsPackage, ObjectivesPackage |

       Design Questions Resolved

       | Question                                               | Answer        
                                      |
       
  |--------------------------------------------------------|--------------------
  -------------------
       -------------------------------|
       | Should LLM generate package_id/workflow_id/created_at? | No - inject in
  P5.3 persistence layer 
                                      |
       | Should Step 2 include IR category?                     | Yes - allow IR
  as optional (empty list
        if no interface requirements) |
       | Should Step 3 include INTF category?                   | Yes - allow
  INTF as optional (derived 
       from IR if present)            |

       State Flow (P5.2 output only - no persistence)

       Pass 1 (DEFINITION):
         generate_step_output() returns:
           {"v1": {"data_sheet": "...", "todo_list": "...", ...}, "v2": {...},
  "v3": {...}}
         → stored in state.methodology[step_key] by existing advance_node()

       Pass 2 (EXECUTION):
         generate_step_output() returns:
           {"stakeholders": [...], "constraints": {...}, ...}  # JSON dict
         → stored in state.artifacts[step_key] by existing advance_node()

       Note: DB persistence of these dicts is handled by P5.3, not P5.2.

       # Unit tests for prompt templates
       pytest apps/api/tests/unit/test_prompts.py -v

       # Manual verification: run single prompt
       python -c "from orchestration.prompts import get_prompt;
  print(get_prompt('problem_definition', 
       'v1', 'data_sheet'))"

       Gate A verification (tools/verify_methodology.py) is P6.1 scope.

       Risks & Mitigations

       | Risk                        | Mitigation
  |
       
  |-----------------------------|-----------------------------------------------
  -------------|
       | LLM output quality          | Explicit structure in prompts, use
  exemplar format         |
       | JSON parse failures         | Strip markdown fences, clear error
  messages, retry prompt  |
       | Token limits for 12 prompts | Each prompt is independent; can batch or
  parallelize later |
       | Context too large for V3    | Summarize or truncate prior docs if
  needed                 |

       Negative Patterns to Avoid

       - Do NOT add Pydantic models to apps/api/domain/ (keep pure)
       - Do NOT modify apps/api/infrastructure/llm.py (P5.1 stable)
       - Do NOT implement artifact persistence (P5.3)
       - Do NOT create Gate A tooling (P6.1)
       - Do NOT allow prose in Pass 2 outputs (JSON only)

       Constraints Honored

       - Doc 1 methodology (V1→V2→V3, 4 docs per step)
       - Pass 1 autonomous (gate policy controls interrupts)
       - Pass 2 human-gated
       - Flat layout (apps/api/)
       - Use LLM adapter from P5.1 (unchanged)
       - Traceability IDs (SH-, FR-, CAP-*)
       - Scope-tight to P5.2 only

✦ I have reviewed Co-Developer-1's evaluation.

  Evaluation:
  Co-Developer-1 raises a valid point about Metadata Injection. Doc 3 schemas
  include fields like package_id, workflow_id, created_at. The LLM cannot and
  should not generate these.

  Clarification Decision:
   1. Content-Only Output: The LLM prompts for Pass 2 should request the content
      fields of the package (e.g., canonical_problem_definition, stakeholders),
      not the metadata fields.
   2. Wrapper Responsibility: The persistence layer (P5.3) or the node logic
      (P5.2 generate_step_output) will be responsible for wrapping this content
      into the full Pydantic model and injecting IDs/timestamps. Since P5.2 is
      "no persistence", generate_step_output will return the content dict. The
      state update logic in nodes.py (or advance_node) will implicitly hold this
      content until P5.3 handles the full packaging.

  State Flow Example Correction:
   * Current Plan Example: {"stakeholders": [...], "constraints": {...}}
   * Corrected Example: {"canonical_problem_definition": {...}, "stakeholders":
     [...], "constraints": {...}} (Matching Doc 3 content fields).

│ ## Working Model                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ - **Per‑Project Git**: Each folder in `projects/` is its own repo. Do normal │
│ branching and PRs per project.                                               │
│ - **Documentation‑First**: Capture decisions in `docs/` and project READMEs. │
│ Prefer small, composable guides and templates (see                           │
│ `projects/ai-env/templates/`).                                               │
│ - **Mirrors for Transfer**: Keep `prompts/`, `scripts/`, and `workflows/`    │
│ both as reusable libraries and as environment mirrors inside                 │
│ `projects/ai-env` to support iteration and feedback loops.                   │
│  - **Agents**: See `AGENTS.md` for CLI agent expectations, safety, and the   │
│ mirror → canonical → consumer workflow.                                      │

│ # Architecture Decisions Log                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ This log records approved deviations from specification documents per        │
│ docs/spec/0_SOLVER-Development-Directive.md.                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ## 2026-01-02 - P2.3                                                         │
│ **Deviation:** Use custom SolverCheckpointSaver instead of                   │
│ langgraph.checkpoint.postgres.PostgresSaver                                  │
│ **Document:** docs/spec/3_solver-technical-spec.md                           │
│ **Reason:** Installed langgraph-checkpoint-postgres v3.0.2 expects           │
│ columns/tables absent from 001_initial_schema.py (checkpoint_ns,             │
│ checkpoint_blobs, checkpoint_writes.type, checkpoint_writes.task_path, BYTEA │
│ storage); migrations are out of scope for P2.                                │
│ **Approved by:** Architect                                                   │
│ **Incorporated into spec:** docs/spec/3_solver-technical-spec.md updated to  │
│ reference SolverCheckpointSaver and Gate D test changes.                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ## 2026-01-03 - P3.3                                                         │
│ **Deviation:** Clear human_decision after routing instead of in              │
│ process_decision_node                                                        │
│ **Document:** docs/spec/3_solver-technical-spec.md §8.4                      │
│ **Reason:** Doc 3 §8.4 clears human_decision in process_decision_node, but   │
│ §8.2 route_after_decision reads it AFTER that node runs. Clearing before     │
│ routing causes graph to default to "end" instead of correct branch.          │
│ Resolution: clear in advance_node (approve path), structure_node (reject     │
│ path), and validate_node (modify path).                                      │
│ **Approved by:** Co-Developer review + plan approval                         │
│ **Incorporated into spec:** Updated docs/spec/3_solver-technical-spec.md     │
│ §8.4 process_decision_node and advance_node.                                 │

│ ### 13.5 Document Versions                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ```                                                                          │
│ V1: Initial creation (forward dependencies only)                             │
│ V2: First refinement (cross-document feedback)                               │
│ V3: Final refinement (authoritative for Pass 2)                              │
│ ```                                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ ### 13.6 Pass 1 Gate Policies                                                │
│                                                                              │
│ ```                                                                          │
│ none:        No gates, fully autonomous                                      │
│ per_step:    Gate after each step's V3 (default interactive)                 │
│ end_of_pass: Gate only after all 10 steps complete                           │
│ ```                                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ ### 13.7 Instance Types                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ ```                                                                          │
│ Instance 0:  Universal       "Constitution"   Meta-methodology               │
│ Instance 1:  Implementation  "Institution"    Enforcement mechanism          │
│ Instance N:  Domain          "Practice"       Substantive knowledge          │
│ ```                                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ ### 13.8 API Endpoints Summary                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ ```                                                                          │
│ POST   /workflow                    Create new workflow                      │
│ GET    /workflow/{id}               Get workflow state                       │
│ POST   /workflow/{id}/clarify       Submit clarification answers             │
│ POST   /workflow/{id}/resume        Submit human decision                    │
│ (approve/reject/modify)                                                      │
│ GET    /workflow/{id}/stream        SSE stream for real-time updates         │
│ DELETE /workflow/{id}               Abandon workflow                         │
│ ```                                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ ### 13.9 SSE Event Types                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ```                                                                          │
│ workflow.started:              Workflow execution began                      │
│ workflow.completed:            Workflow finished                             │
│ step.started:                  Step execution began                          │
│ step.awaiting_clarification:   Step needs user input                         │
│ step.awaiting_review:          Step output ready for approval                │
│ step.approved:                 Step approved by human                        │
│ step.revision_requested:       Step revision requested                       │
│ artifact.delta:                Streaming output chunk                        │
│ artifact.final:                Final artifact ready                          │
│ error:                         Error occurred                                │
│ ```                                                                          │

│ ```python                                                                    │
│ async def test_audit_trail_complete():                                       │
│     # Complete full workflow                                                 │
│     workflow = await create_and_complete_workflow()                          │
│                                                                              │
│     # Query audit log                                                        │
│     audit_entries = await get_audit_log(workflow.workflow_id)                │
│                                                                              │
│     # Verify completeness                                                    │
│     assert len(audit_entries) >= 12  # At least 4 transitions × 3 steps      │
│                                                                              │
│     # Verify ordering                                                        │
│     timestamps = [e.created_at for e in audit_entries]                       │
│     assert timestamps == sorted(timestamps)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│     # Verify all steps represented                                           │
│     steps = set(e.step_name for e in audit_entries if e.step_name)           │
│     assert StepName.PROBLEM_DEFINITION in steps                              │
│     assert StepName.REQUIREMENTS in steps                                    │
│     assert StepName.OBJECTIVES in steps                                      │
│                                                                              │
│     # Verify terminal states                                                 │
│     approved_events = [e for e in audit_entries if e.to_status ==            │
│ 'approved']                                                                  │
│     assert len(approved_events) == 3  # One per step                         │
│ ```                                                                          │

> Generate a plan for P5.3 - Artifact Storage for the Senior Developer to
  implement.

  Responding with gemini-3-pro-preview
✦ Plan: Slice P5.3 (Artifact Storage)

  Objective: Implement persistence logic to store Pass 1 methodology documents
  and Pass 2 artifact packages into the artifacts table, injecting necessary
  metadata. Add schema validation using the deferred Pydantic models.

  Scope:
   - In Scope:
       - apps/api/domain/packages.py: Pydantic models for Step 1-3 packages
         (matching Doc 3 schemas).
       - apps/api/application/workflow_service.py: Update sync_db_from_state to
         persist artifacts.
       - apps/api/infrastructure/db/repositories/artifact.py: Ensure repository
         handles upserts correctly.
       - Gate B partial satisfaction (packages validate).
   - Out of Scope:
       - Traceability linking (P5.4).
       - Gate A verification script (P6.1).

  Files to Create/Modify:

   1. `apps/api/domain/packages.py` (New)
       - Define Pydantic models for ProblemDefinitionPackage,
         RequirementsPackage, ObjectivesPackage.
       - Ensure fields strictly match docs/spec/3_solver-technical-spec.md and
         packages/contracts/*.schema.json.
       - Include metadata fields (package_id, workflow_id, etc.) as optional in
         the model (for reading) or handled separately. Correction: The LLM
         returns content-only. The Domain Model should represent the full
         artifact including metadata. We need a Content model (LLM output) and a
         Package model (Database record). Or simply make metadata fields
         optional/defaulted.

   2. `apps/api/application/workflow_service.py`
       - Modify `sync_db_from_state`:
           - Pass 1 (Methodology): Iterate state.methodology. For each
             step/version/doc_type:
               - Construct Artifact domain object.
               - artifact_type="methodology_doc".
               - content = markdown string.
               - Call artifact_repo.save().
           - Pass 2 (Execution): Iterate state.artifacts. For each step:
               - Get content dict.
               - Validate: Instantiate Pydantic model (e.g.,
                 ProblemDefinitionPackage) with content + injected metadata.
                 Fail if invalid.
               - Construct Artifact domain object.
               - artifact_type="step_package".
               - content = Pydantic model .model_dump(mode='json').
               - Call artifact_repo.save().

   3. `apps/api/infrastructure/db/repositories/artifact.py`
       - Ensure save method performs an upsert (update if exists for
         workflow/step/pass/type) to handle re-runs/revisions.

  Detailed Implementation Strategy:

   * Pydantic Models: These are the "Gatekeepers". If the LLM output doesn't
     match, we catch it here before DB save.
       * Step1Content, Step2Content, Step3Content (matching LLM output).
       * Step1Package, Step2Package, Step3Package (inherits Content, adds
         metadata).
   * Metadata Injection:
       * package_id: Generate new UUID if not exists.
       * workflow_id: From state.
       * created_at: Current time.
       * step_number: From enum mapping.

  Verification:
   * pytest apps/api/tests/unit/test_packages.py: Verify Pydantic validation
     rules.
   * pytest apps/api/tests/integration/test_artifact_persistence.py: Run a
     workflow (mocked LLM or manual state injection) and verify artifacts table
     is populated correctly.

  Negative Patterns to Avoid:
   * storing raw LLM string: Pass 2 output MUST be parsed JSON stored in
     content_jsonb column.
   * missing IDs: Ensure package_id and workflow_id are populated.

  I am ready to instruct the Senior Developer.

  Responding with gemini-3-pro-preview
✦ P5.3 Implementation Plan: Artifact Storage (Consolidated)

  Objective:
  Implement the persistence layer for methodology documents and step artifacts,
  enforcing Doc 3 schema validation and injecting system metadata. This
  satisfies Gate A (Methodology exists) and Gate B (Schema validation).

  Scope (Strict):
   * In Scope:
       * Full JSON Schema definitions for Steps 1-3.
       * ArtifactService for storage logic and metadata injection.
       * Integration with WorkflowService.sync_db_from_state.
       * jsonschema library integration.
       * Unit and Integration tests for storage logic.
   * Out of Scope:
       * Traceability link extraction (P5.4).
       * Gate Verification Scripts (P6.1).
       * Changes to LLM adapter or Prompts.

  Files to Create/Modify:

   1. `apps/api/pyproject.toml`
       * Action: Add jsonschema>=4.20.0.

   2. `packages/contracts/*.schema.json`
       * Action: Replace stubs with full schemas from Doc 3 (§5.1, §5.2, §5.3).
       * Key Fields: Ensure metadata block (package_id, workflow_id, etc.) and
         content block match exactly.

   3. `apps/api/application/schemas.py` (New)
       * Purpose: Singleton loader for JSON schemas.
       * Logic: Load schemas from packages/contracts on import to avoid I/O per
         request.

   4. `apps/api/application/artifact_service.py` (New)
       * Purpose: Domain service for artifact lifecycle.
       * Method `store_methodology_docs`:
           * Input: methodology_dict (from state).
           * Logic: Flatten the nested V1/V2/V3 dict into 12 Artifact records.
           * Type: methodology_doc.
       * Method `store_step_package`:
           * Input: content_dict (from LLM), workflow_id, step_name.
           * Logic:
               1. Generate package_id (UUID) and created_at.
               2. Construct full package dict (Metadata + Content).
               3. Validate against loaded schema using jsonschema.validate.
                  Raise SchemaValidationError on failure.
               4. Save to DB via ArtifactRepository (upsert).
               5. Return Artifact entity.

   5. `apps/api/application/workflow_service.py`
       * Action: Update sync_db_from_state.
       * Logic:
           * After saving StepExecution:
           * If step.status == APPROVED:
               * Call artifact_service.store_methodology_docs (if Definition
                 pass).
               * Call artifact_service.store_step_package (if Execution pass).
               * Update step_execution.latest_artifact_id with the result.

   6. `apps/api/tests/unit/test_artifact_service.py` (New)
       * Tests:
           * test_validation_failure: Ensure invalid LLM output is rejected.
           * test_metadata_injection: Ensure IDs/timestamps are added.
           * test_methodology_fanout: Ensure 12 docs are created.

  Negative Patterns to Avoid:
   * Validation in API Layer: Validation must happen in the Service layer before
     persistence, not in the route handler.
   * Silent Failures: Validation errors must raise exceptions that stop the
     persistence transaction (rolling back the state update if possible, or at
     least alerting the user).
   * Hardcoded Schemas: Do not define schemas in Python dicts. Must load from
     packages/contracts/*.schema.json to maintain the Single Source of Truth.
   * Ignoring Metadata: Do not save the "raw" LLM content as the package. It
     must be wrapped in the schema's root structure.

  Command: "Begin slice P5.3"

    P5.3 Plan: Artifact Storage

    - Files to create/modify
        - apps/api/application/artifact_service.py (implement storage +
          validation)
        - apps/api/application/schemas.py (schema loader/validator)
        - apps/api/application/workflow_service.py (call artifact service from
          sync_db_from_state)
        - apps/api/orchestration/nodes.py (upgrade validate_output to full
  schema
          validation)
        - apps/api/pyproject.toml (add jsonschema>=4.20.0)
        - packages/contracts/problem_definition.schema.json
        - packages/contracts/requirements.schema.json
        - packages/contracts/objectives.schema.json
        - packages/contracts/README.md (align schema path if needed)
        - apps/api/tests/unit/test_artifact_service.py (new)
        - apps/api/tests/integration/test_artifact_persistence.py (new or extend
          existing)
    - Approach (3–5 bullets)
        1. Implement an ArtifactService that persists methodology docs (Pass 1)
           and step packages (Pass 2), with metadata injection and revision
           tracking.
        2. Load and validate JSON schemas from packages/contracts/*.schema.json
           using jsonschema, and record validation status/errors on artifacts.
        3. Update sync_db_from_state to persist artifacts idempotently based on
           state: store Pass 1 docs when output exists (including at review
           interrupt) and Pass 2 packages only after approval.
        4. Replace placeholder validation in validate_output with full schema
           validation (injecting metadata into a copy for validation).
        5. Add unit + integration tests for storage, idempotency, metadata
           injection, and latest artifact pointer updates.

    Implementation details

    - Schema source of truth: Doc 3 is authoritative for schema paths. Update
      packages/contracts/*.schema.json and align packages/contracts/README.md to
      match (don’t split between schemas/ and root).
    - Schema loader: apps/api/application/schemas.py loads all three schemas
  once
      (module-level). If files are missing, raise a clear error (do not silently
      accept empty stubs once we’ve populated them).
    - Metadata injection (Pass 2 only):
        - package_id: set to the artifact row UUID (best traceability) by
  creating
          the artifact with a pre-generated UUID and reusing it in content.
        - workflow_id: external workflow ID string (not DB UUID).
        - instance_id: UUID string from workflow record.
        - step_number, version, created_at: use step_state.step_number,
  revision,
          and ISO-8601 UTC timestamp.
    - Idempotency + revisions:
        - Methodology docs: use get_methodology_doc(...) to avoid duplicates per
          (workflow_id, step_name, doc_type, doc_version).
        - Step packages: compare content against latest revision; only create a
          new revision if content changed.
    - Storage timing:
        - Pass 1: persist when the methodology output exists (including at
          awaiting_review), and also on auto-advance (gate policy none/
          end_of_pass) by scanning state.methodology for missing docs.
        - Pass 2: persist only after approval (state.artifacts populated by
          advance_node).
    - StepExecution.latest_artifact_id:
        - Set after storing step packages; for methodology docs, set to the last
          stored doc (e.g., V3 detailed procedure) or leave null if we want it
  to
          mean “package only.” Decide and be consistent in tests.
    - Validation:
        - Use full schema validation in validate_output by injecting metadata
  into
          a copy so it conforms to Doc 3 schemas.
        - Persist validation result to artifact fields validation_status and
          schema_version.

    Negative patterns to avoid

    - Do not store Pass 1 docs only after approval; Doc 2 requires storage
  before
      the gate.
    - Do not persist raw LLM output without metadata injection.
    - Do not let sync_db_from_state create duplicate artifacts on resume/
      interrupt.
    - Do not introduce new schema paths or generate code into
  packages/contracts/
      generated/.
    - Do not perform validation in route handlers; keep it in service/node
  logic.

    Open questions (max 3)

    1. Should package_id equal the artifact row UUID? (recommended for
       traceability)
    2. Should latest_artifact_id be set for Pass 1 docs or only for Pass 2
       packages?
    3. Do you want schema path alignment by updating
  packages/contracts/README.md
       to match Doc 3 (root-level schemas)?
  </Co-Developer-1>

  Responding with gemini-3-pro-preview
✦ I have evaluated Co-Developer-1's plan.

  Evaluation:
  Co-Developer-1's plan is superior to my previous consolidated plan because it
  addresses nuances of the persistence lifecycle (idempotency, timing, revision
  tracking) that I glossed over.

  Key Improvements:
   1. Storage Timing: The distinction that Pass 1 docs should be stored before
      the gate (so the human can review them) is critical. My previous plan
      implied storage only on APPROVED transition, which would make the review
      screen empty.
   2. Idempotency: Explicitly handling re-runs/resumes to avoid duplicate
      artifacts is essential for a robust system.
   3. Metadata Injection: Reusing the Artifact UUID as the package_id is a smart
      normalization choice.
   4. Validation Location: Updating nodes.py:validate_output to use the schema
      validator means we catch errors during execution (providing feedback to
      the agent) rather than just at persistence time (which would crash the
      system).

  Reflected Decision:
  I will adopt Co-Developer-1's approach, specifically the timing logic (store
  Pass 1 early) and the validation integration in nodes.py.

  Addressing Open Questions:
   1. package_id = artifact.id? Yes. This creates a hard link between the DB
      record and the JSON content.
   2. latest_artifact_id for Pass 1? Yes, point to the V3 Detailed Procedure
      (the final executable spec). This gives the UI a logical "current
      document" to display.
   3. Schema Path Alignment? Yes. packages/contracts/*.schema.json at the root
      of that package is the correct location per Doc 3. The generated/ folder
      in the current file tree should be ignored or deleted if empty.

  Revised Consolidated Plan (P5.3 - Final):

  Objective: Implement Artifact persistence (Pass 1 & 2), schema validation
  (using jsonschema), and metadata injection.

  Files:
   1. apps/api/pyproject.toml (+jsonschema)
   2. packages/contracts/*.schema.json (Full Doc 3 schemas)
   3. apps/api/application/schemas.py (Loader)
   4. apps/api/application/artifact_service.py (Storage logic + Validation)
   5. apps/api/application/workflow_service.py (Integration)
   6. apps/api/orchestration/nodes.py (Call validation)
   7. apps/api/tests/unit/test_artifact_service.py (Tests)

  Logic Flow:
   1. Validation: nodes.py calls artifact_service.validate_content(content,
      step, pass). Service injects dummy metadata, validates against schema,
      returns result.
   2. Pass 1 Storage: sync_db_from_state calls store_methodology whenever
      state.methodology changes (idempotent upsert).
   3. Pass 2 Storage: sync_db_from_state calls store_package when step.status ==
      APPROVED.
   4. Metadata: store_package generates UUID, injects into content package_id,
      saves to DB artifact.id.

> Review this proposal from Co-Developer-1 and whether you agree or disagree
  explain yourself.

  <Co-Developer-1>
  Mostly aligned — the Senior Developer accepted the critical corrections
    (Pass‑1 timing, idempotency, package_id reuse, schema‑loader errors,
  metadata
    injection for validation). That matches my directives.

    Key gaps/risks still to close:

    - Artifact persistence should not rely on step_state after advance. After
      approval, the graph resets step_state to the next step, so storing
      “completed_step” from step_state will miss the prior step’s package/
      methodology. Best fix: in sync_db_from_state, scan state.methodology and
      state.artifacts for any missing DB records and persist idempotently.
    - Schema path consistency still needs explicit resolution. Doc 3 uses
      root‑level packages/contracts/*.schema.json; README currently says
  schemas/.
      Choose one source of truth (Doc 3), update README accordingly, and avoid
      introducing a new path.
    - Idempotency specifics must be implemented, not just stated:
        - Methodology docs: check (workflow_id, step_name, doc_type,
  doc_version)
          before insert.
        - Step packages: compare content (or hash) vs latest revision; only bump
          revision if content changed.
    - Validation must not mutate state. Inject metadata into a copy, validate,
  and
      keep the state’s content‑only dict intact. Ensure created_at is ISO‑8601
      string, workflow_id is the external ID, and instance_id is the UUID
  string.
    - Gate B scope: schema validation is P5.3; traceability is P5.4. Don’t claim
      trace links in P5.3.

    Best path to the objective:

    - Implement persistence by scanning state.methodology and state.artifacts
  with
      idempotent inserts; persist Pass‑1 docs when output exists (including at
      review interrupts) and Pass‑2 packages only after approval.
    - Keep the schema source of truth aligned (Doc 3 path) and enforce
  validation
      via metadata‑injected copies.
    - Use latest_artifact_id as the “final output pointer” per pass (V3
      detailed_procedure for Pass‑1; package for Pass‑2), as the Senior
  Developer
      suggests.

    If these are incorporated, the approach will be spec‑correct and robust for
    Gate A/B.

✦ I have reviewed Co-Developer-1's critique of the Senior Developer's P5.3 plan.

  Evaluation:
  Co-Developer-1 identifies a subtle but critical bug in the proposed
  persistence logic: relying on step_state after the graph advances will indeed
  point to the next step (initialized to empty), causing the system to miss
  saving the completed step's artifact.

  Corrective Action:
  The persistence logic (sync_db_from_state) must not look at current_step or
  step_state to decide what to save. Instead, it must iterate over the
  accumulated state.methodology and state.artifacts dictionaries and sync them
  to the DB (Idempotency is key here).

  Agreement:
  I fully agree with Co-Developer-1.
   1. State Sync Bug: Fixed by scanning accumulated state.
   2. Schema Path: Doc 3 is authoritative.
   3. Idempotency: Must implement content hashing or lookup-before-insert.
   4. Validation: Must validate a copy, not mutate the state object.

  Recommendation:
  Approve the plan with the explicit instruction to implement the State Scan
  persistence strategy rather than the Transition Trigger strategy.

       P5.3 Implementation Plan: Artifact Storage

       Overview

       Implement artifact persistence for SOLVER workflow engine. Store Pass 1
  methodology docs (12 per 
       step) and Pass 2 step packages (JSON) with schema validation.

       Gates Addressed:
       - Gate A: 36 methodology docs exist (3 steps × 12 docs)
       - Gate B: Packages validate against schema (trace links are P5.4)

       Current State (P5.2 Complete)

       - nodes.py: generate_step_output() generates artifacts, advance_node()
  stores to state
         - Pass 1: state.methodology[step_key] = {"v1": {...}, "v2": {...},
  "v3": {...}}
         - Pass 2: state.artifacts[step_key] = content-only JSON dict
       - workflow_service.py: sync_db_from_state() syncs position/step but NOT
  artifacts (line 532-612)
       - Schema contracts: Empty stubs in packages/contracts/*.schema.json (Doc
  3 path is authoritative)
       - DB: artifacts table exists with dual-purpose storage (methodology_doc /
  step_package)
       - Models/Repos: Artifact model and ArtifactRepository exist but unused

       Critical Design: Scan-Based Persistence

       Problem: After approval, advance_node() resets step_state to the NEXT
  step. If we rely on 
       step_state to determine "which step completed," we'll miss the prior
  step's artifacts.

       Solution: In sync_db_from_state(), scan state.methodology and
  state.artifacts dictionaries for 
       entries that lack corresponding DB records. Persist idempotently. This
  handles:
       - Resume after crash during persistence
       - Auto-advance with gate policy NONE/END_OF_PASS
       - Multiple sync calls on same state

       Files to Create

       | File                                         | Purpose
  |
       
  |----------------------------------------------|------------------------------
  -------|
       | apps/api/application/artifact_service.py     | Artifact storage &
  validation logic |
       | apps/api/application/schemas.py              | JSON schema loader
  |
       | apps/api/tests/unit/test_artifact_service.py | Unit tests for artifact
  service     |

       Files to Modify

       | File                                              | Changes            
                                        |
       
  |---------------------------------------------------|-------------------------
  -------------------
       ---------------------------------|
       | apps/api/application/workflow_service.py          | Add
  sync_artifacts_from_state(), call from 
       sync_db_from_state()             |
       | apps/api/orchestration/nodes.py                   | Upgrade
  validate_output() to full schema 
       validation with metadata injection |
       | apps/api/pyproject.toml                           | Add
  jsonschema>=4.20.0 dependency          
                                        |
       | packages/contracts/problem_definition.schema.json | Full schema per Doc
  3 §5.1                 
                                        |
       | packages/contracts/requirements.schema.json       | Full schema per Doc
  3 §5.2                 
                                        |
       | packages/contracts/objectives.schema.json         | Full schema per Doc
  3 §5.3

       | packages/contracts/README.md                      | Align schema path
  to Doc 3 (root-level, not
        schemas/)                       |

       Implementation Phases

       Phase 1: Schema Contracts

       Replace stub schemas with full definitions per Doc 3.

       problem_definition.schema.json required fields:
       - Metadata: package_id, workflow_id, instance_id, step_number, version,
  created_at
       - Content: title, canonical_problem_definition, stakeholders[],
  constraints, scope, 
       success_criteria[]
       - ID patterns: SH-\d{3}, HC-\d{3}, SC-\d{3}, CRT-\d{3}, IN-\d{3},
  OUT-\d{3}, IP-\d{3}, ASM-\d{3}

       requirements.schema.json required fields:
       - Metadata (same)
       - Content: overview, requirements[], coverage_map[]
       - ID patterns: (FR|NFR|CR|IR)-\d{3}
       - Categories: FR, NFR, CR, IR (IR optional)

       objectives.schema.json required fields:
       - Metadata (same)
       - Content: overview, objectives[], success_framework, trace_map[]
       - ID patterns: (CAP|QUAL|COMP|INTF)-\d{3}
       - Categories: CAP, QUAL, COMP, INTF (INTF optional)

       Phase 2: Schema Loader (schemas.py)

       SCHEMA_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.parent / "packages" /
  "contracts"

       def _load_schema(filename: str) -> dict:
           """Load schema or raise clear error if missing."""
           path = SCHEMA_DIR / filename
           if not path.exists():
               raise FileNotFoundError(f"Schema not found: {path}. Ensure
  packages/contracts/ contains 
       schemas per Doc 3.")
           with open(path) as f:
               return json.load(f)

       PROBLEM_DEFINITION_SCHEMA =
  _load_schema("problem_definition.schema.json")
       REQUIREMENTS_SCHEMA = _load_schema("requirements.schema.json")
       OBJECTIVES_SCHEMA = _load_schema("objectives.schema.json")

       Phase 3: Artifact Service (artifact_service.py)

       class ArtifactService:
           async def store_methodology_docs(
               self,
               workflow_id: UUID,
               step_name: StepName,
               methodology_output: dict,  # {"v1": {...}, "v2": {...}, "v3":
  {...}}
           ) -> list[Artifact]:
               """Store 12 methodology docs (4 doc_types × 3 versions)
  idempotently.

               Idempotency: Check (workflow_id, step_name, doc_type,
  doc_version) before insert.
               Skip if already exists.

               Creates Artifact records with:
               - artifact_type='methodology_doc'
               - document_type, document_version, content_markdown
               """

           async def store_step_package(
               self,
               workflow_id: UUID,
               instance_id: str,
               workflow_external_id: str,
               step_name: StepName,
               package_content: dict,  # Content-only dict from LLM
               validate: bool = True,
           ) -> Artifact:
               """Store Pass 2 package with metadata injection and validation.

               Idempotency: Compare content against latest revision; only create
  new
               revision if content changed.

               Metadata injection (into COPY, not original):
               - package_id: Pre-generate artifact UUID, use for both content
  and row ID
               - workflow_id: External workflow ID string (not DB UUID)
               - instance_id: UUID string from workflow record
               - step_number: From STEP_NUMBERS mapping
               - version: Revision number (starts at 1)
               - created_at: ISO-8601 UTC timestamp

               Validation: Inject metadata into copy, validate against schema.
               Storage: Create Artifact with artifact_type='step_package',
  content_jsonb.
               """

       Phase 4: Workflow Service Integration

       Add to workflow_service.py:

       async def sync_artifacts_from_state(
           self,
           workflow_id: str,
           workflow_db_id: UUID,
           state: WorkflowState,
       ) -> dict[str, UUID]:
           """Scan state dictionaries and persist any missing artifacts.

           Returns dict mapping step_key to latest artifact_id created.

           Pass 1 (methodology): Persist when output exists (including at review
  interrupt).
           Pass 2 (packages): Persist only for APPROVED steps.
           """
           result = {}

           # Pass 1: Scan state.methodology for missing docs
           for step_key, methodology_output in state.methodology.items():
               step_name = StepName(step_key)
               # Check if docs already persisted (idempotent)
               existing = await
  self._artifact_repo.list_methodology_docs(workflow_db_id, step_name)
               if len(existing) < 12:  # Missing some docs
                   artifacts = await
  self._artifact_service.store_methodology_docs(
                       workflow_id=workflow_db_id,
                       step_name=step_name,
                       methodology_output=methodology_output,
                   )
                   if artifacts:
                       result[step_key] = artifacts[-1].id  # V3
  detailed_procedure

           # Pass 2: Scan state.artifacts for approved steps needing persistence
           for step_key, package_content in state.artifacts.items():
               step_name = StepName(step_key)
               # Only persist if step is approved (check step_executions table)
               step_exec = await self._step_repo.get_by_composite(
                   workflow_id=workflow_db_id,
                   pass_type=PassType.EXECUTION,
                   step_name=step_name,
               )
               if step_exec and step_exec.status == StepStatus.APPROVED:
                   # Check if package already persisted (idempotent)
                   existing = await
  self._artifact_repo.get_step_package(workflow_db_id, step_name)
                   if existing is None:
                       artifact = await
  self._artifact_service.store_step_package(
                           workflow_id=workflow_db_id,
                           instance_id=state.instance_id,
                           workflow_external_id=state.workflow_id,
                           step_name=step_name,
                           package_content=package_content,
                       )
                       result[step_key] = artifact.id

           return result

       Modify sync_db_from_state() to call artifact sync at end:

       # At end of sync_db_from_state(), after step_execution updates:
       artifact_ids = await self.sync_artifacts_from_state(
           workflow_id=workflow_id,
           workflow_db_id=workflow.id,
           state=state,
       )

       # Update latest_artifact_id for any newly persisted artifacts
       for step_key, artifact_id in artifact_ids.items():
           step_name = StepName(step_key)
           # Determine pass type based on which dict the artifact came from
           pass_type = PassType.DEFINITION if step_key in state.methodology else
  PassType.EXECUTION
           step_exec = await self._step_repo.get_by_composite(workflow.id,
  pass_type, step_name)
           if step_exec:
               step_exec.latest_artifact_id = artifact_id

       Phase 5: Node Validation Upgrade (nodes.py)

       Upgrade validate_output() for Pass 2 to use full schema validation:

       async def validate_output(step: StepName, output: dict, pass_type:
  PassType = None) -> 
       ValidationResult:
           """Validate step output.

           Pass 1: Basic structural validation (required keys exist).
           Pass 2: Full JSON schema validation with temporary metadata
  injection.
           """
           if output is None:
               return ValidationResult(passed=False, errors=[{"message": "Output
  is None"}])

           if pass_type == PassType.DEFINITION or pass_type is None:
               # Pass 1: Basic key check only
               required_keys = get_required_keys(step)
               missing = [k for k in required_keys if k not in output]
               if missing:
                   return ValidationResult(passed=False, errors=[{"message":
  f"Missing: {missing}"}])
               return ValidationResult(passed=True)

           # Pass 2: Full schema validation
           # Inject temporary metadata into COPY (don't mutate state)
           temp_package = {
               "package_id": "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000",  #
  Placeholder
               "workflow_id": "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000",
               "instance_id": "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000",
               "step_number": STEP_NUMBERS[step],
               "version": 1,
               "created_at": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
               **output,  # Content-only dict from LLM
           }

           schema = _get_schema_for_step(step)
           validator = jsonschema.Draft7Validator(schema)
           errors = [{"path": list(e.path), "message": e.message} for e in 
       validator.iter_errors(temp_package)]

           return ValidationResult(passed=len(errors) == 0, errors=errors if
  errors else None)

       Key Design Decisions

       | Decision                 | Choice              | Rationale             
                  |
       
  |--------------------------|---------------------|----------------------------
  -------------------
       -----------|
       | Scan-based persistence   | Yes                 | Avoids post-advance
  state loss; handles crash 
       recovery   |
       | Pass 1 storage timing    | When output exists  | Doc 2 requires storage
  before gate; enables 
       review       |
       | Pass 2 storage timing    | After approval only | Packages must be
  validated and approved       
                  |
       | Idempotent inserts       | Yes                 | Safe resume/retry on
  crash or multiple sync 
       calls        |
       | package_id = artifact.id | Yes                 | Better traceability;
  simpler queries          
                  |
       | Metadata injection       | Into COPY only      | Don't mutate state;
  preserve content-only 
       pattern        |
       | Schema loader errors     | Raise exception     | Fail fast if schema
  files missing (after 
       implementation) |
       | Separate ArtifactService | Yes                 | Single responsibility,
  testable, reusable     
                  |
       | latest_artifact_id       | Per-pass            | V3 detailed_procedure
  for Pass 1; package for 
       Pass 2     |

  before gate

  resume/interrupt

  Evaluation:
  This plan is excellent. It incorporates all critical feedback:
   1. Scan-Based Persistence: This fundamentally solves the "state loss after
      advance" bug. By scanning the accumulated state.methodology and
      state.artifacts maps, the system ensures eventual consistency regardless
      of where the graph cursor is.

  Responding with gemini-3-pro-preview
✦ Excellent. P5.3 is complete and robust. The 126 passing tests provide strong
  confidence.

  Status:
   - P5.1 (LLM Adapter): Complete (OpenAIResponsesAdapter).
   - P5.2 (Prompts): Complete (V1->V3 prompts, strict JSON).
   - P5.3 (Storage): Complete (ArtifactService, Schema Validation, Scan-based
     Sync).

  Next Slice: P5.4 - Traceability Links

  Objective:
  Extract and persist traceability links from the stored Pass 2 artifacts to
  satisfy the full requirements of Gate B.

  Recall Doc 1 & Doc 3 Traceability Rules:
   * Step 1: No incoming traces (it's the root).
   * Step 2 Requirements: Must trace to Step 1 elements (Stakeholders,
     Constraints, Scope, etc.).
   * Step 3 Objectives: Must trace to Step 2 Requirements.
   * Storage: traceability_links table (Step 7.3 in Doc 3).
   * Extraction: The links are embedded in the JSON content (e.g., traces_to:
     ["SH-001"]). We need to parse these out and save them to the relational
     table for queryability.

  Plan for P5.4:
   1. Service: Implement TraceabilityService.
   2. Logic:
       * extract_links(artifact_content, step_number) -> List[TraceabilityLink].
       * persist_links(links) -> Upsert to DB.
   3. Integration: Hook into ArtifactService.store_step_package. After saving
      the artifact, call traceability_service.process_artifact(artifact).
   4. Validation: Ensure referential integrity (e.g., if a Requirement traces to
      SH-001, does SH-001 exist?). Constraint: Doc 3 might not enforce strict
      foreign keys on trace IDs yet, but the service should log warnings.

  I will formulate the detailed plan for P5.4.

  Shall I present the plan for P5.4?

  made and compare with your directives above.  Reflect on the decisions made
  and give your perspective on what you think is best to achieve the objective.
