   P5.4 Implementation Plan: Traceability Links

   Overview

   Extract and persist traceability links from approved Step 2 and Step 3
  packages. Links connect elements
   across steps (Stakeholders → Requirements → Objectives) to enable full chain
  verification.

   Gate Addressed:
   - Gate B: Packages validate + trace links work

   Current State (P5.3 Complete)

   - ArtifactService.store_step_package() stores Pass 2 packages with content in
  content_jsonb
   - WorkflowService.sync_artifacts_from_state() persists artifacts for APPROVED
  steps
   - TraceabilityLink model and TraceabilityLinkRepository exist but unused
   - TraceabilityService is a stub (comments only)
   - GET /workflows/{id}/traceability endpoint not implemented

   Traceability Chain Structure

   Step 1 (ProblemDefinitionPackage) - Source elements:
   - Stakeholders: SH-001, SH-002, ...
   - Hard Constraints: HC-001, HC-002, ...
   - Soft Constraints: SC-001, SC-002, ...
   - In-Scope: IN-001, IN-002, ...
   - Success Criteria: CRT-001, CRT-002, ...
   - Integration Points: IP-001, IP-002, ...

   Step 2 (RequirementsPackage) - Links to Step 1:
   - requirements[i].source.id → Step 1 element (explicit source)
   - requirements[i].trace.stakeholders[] → SH-xxx IDs
   - requirements[i].trace.constraints[] → HC-xxx/SC-xxx IDs
   - requirements[i].trace.scope_in[] → IN-xxx IDs
   - requirements[i].trace.success_criteria[] → CRT-xxx IDs
   - requirements[i].trace.integration_points[] → IP-xxx IDs
   - coverage_map[i].source_id → Step 1 element

   Step 3 (ObjectivesPackage) - Links to Step 2:
   - objectives[i].linked_requirements[] → FR/NFR/CR/IR-xxx IDs
   - trace_map[i].requirement_ids[] → FR/NFR/CR/IR-xxx IDs
   - backward_trace[i].requirement_id → FR/NFR/CR/IR-xxx ID
   - objectives[i].acceptance_criteria[] → CRT-xxx (back to Step 1)

   Files to Modify

   | File                                         | Changes
  |
   
  |----------------------------------------------|------------------------------
  -------------------|
   | apps/api/application/traceability_service.py | Replace stub with full
  implementation           |
   | apps/api/application/workflow_service.py     | Call TraceabilityService
  after artifact storage |
   | apps/api/routes/workflows.py                 | Add GET
  /{workflow_id}/traceability endpoint    |

   Files to Create

   | File                                             | Purpose
  |
   
  |--------------------------------------------------|--------------------------
  --------------|
   | apps/api/tests/unit/test_traceability_service.py | Unit tests for
  traceability extraction |
   | tools/verify_traces.py                           | Gate B verification
  script             |

   Implementation Phases

   Phase 1: TraceabilityService (traceability_service.py)

   from typing import Any
   from uuid import UUID
   from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

   from domain.state import StepName, STEP_NUMBERS
   from infrastructure.db.models import TraceabilityLink
   from infrastructure.db.repositories import TraceabilityLinkRepository


   class TraceabilityService:
       """Extract and persist traceability links from step packages."""

       def __init__(self, session: AsyncSession) -> None:
           self._session = session
           self._trace_repo = TraceabilityLinkRepository(session)

       async def extract_and_persist_links(
           self,
           workflow_id: UUID,
           step_name: StepName,
           package_content: dict[str, Any],
       ) -> list[TraceabilityLink]:
           """Extract trace links from package and persist idempotently.

           Args:
               workflow_id: Database workflow UUID.
               step_name: Step name (REQUIREMENTS or OBJECTIVES).
               package_content: Full package dict with metadata.

           Returns:
               List of created TraceabilityLink records.
           """
           step_number = STEP_NUMBERS[step_name]

           # Step 1 has no forward links (it's the source)
           if step_name == StepName.PROBLEM_DEFINITION:
               return []

           links = []

           if step_name == StepName.REQUIREMENTS:
               links = self._extract_step2_links(step_number, package_content)
           elif step_name == StepName.OBJECTIVES:
               links = self._extract_step3_links(step_number, package_content)

           # Persist idempotently
           created = []
           for link_data in links:
               existing = await self._trace_repo.find_by_unique(
                   workflow_id=workflow_id,
                   from_id=link_data["from_id"],
                   to_id=link_data["to_id"],
                   link_type=link_data["link_type"],
               )
               if existing:
                   continue

               link = TraceabilityLink(
                   workflow_id=workflow_id,
                   from_step=link_data["from_step"],
                   from_type=link_data["from_type"],
                   from_id=link_data["from_id"],
                   to_step=link_data["to_step"],
                   to_type=link_data["to_type"],
                   to_id=link_data["to_id"],
                   link_type=link_data["link_type"],
                   consolidated=link_data.get("consolidated", False),
               )
               self._session.add(link)
               created.append(link)

           if created:
               await self._session.flush()

           return created

       def _extract_step2_links(
           self,
           step_number: int,
           package: dict[str, Any],
       ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
           """Extract Step 1 → Step 2 links from RequirementsPackage."""
           links = []

           for req in package.get("requirements", []):
               req_id = req.get("id")
               if not req_id:
                   continue

               req_type = self._get_type_from_id(req_id)

               # source.id → primary derivation
               source = req.get("source", {})
               if source.get("id"):
                   links.append({
                       "from_step": 1,
                       "from_type": source.get("type", "unknown"),
                       "from_id": source["id"],
                       "to_step": step_number,
                       "to_type": req_type,
                       "to_id": req_id,
                       "link_type": "derives",
                   })

               # trace object → additional links
               trace = req.get("trace", {})

               for sh_id in trace.get("stakeholders", []):
                   links.append({
                       "from_step": 1,
                       "from_type": "stakeholder",
                       "from_id": sh_id,
                       "to_step": step_number,
                       "to_type": req_type,
                       "to_id": req_id,
                       "link_type": "traces_to",
                   })

               for const_id in trace.get("constraints", []):
                   const_type = "hard_constraint" if const_id.startswith("HC-")
  else "soft_constraint"
                   links.append({
                       "from_step": 1,
                       "from_type": const_type,
                       "from_id": const_id,
                       "to_step": step_number,
                       "to_type": req_type,
                       "to_id": req_id,
                       "link_type": "traces_to",
                   })

               for scope_id in trace.get("scope_in", []):
                   links.append({
                       "from_step": 1,
                       "from_type": "scope_in",
                       "from_id": scope_id,
                       "to_step": step_number,
                       "to_type": req_type,
                       "to_id": req_id,
                       "link_type": "traces_to",
                   })

               for crt_id in trace.get("success_criteria", []):
                   links.append({
                       "from_step": 1,
                       "from_type": "success_criterion",
                       "from_id": crt_id,
                       "to_step": step_number,
                       "to_type": req_type,
                       "to_id": req_id,
                       "link_type": "traces_to",
                   })

               for ip_id in trace.get("integration_points", []):
                   links.append({
                       "from_step": 1,
                       "from_type": "integration_point",
                       "from_id": ip_id,
                       "to_step": step_number,
                       "to_type": req_type,
                       "to_id": req_id,
                       "link_type": "traces_to",
                   })

           return links

       def _extract_step3_links(
           self,
           step_number: int,
           package: dict[str, Any],
       ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
           """Extract Step 2 → Step 3 links from ObjectivesPackage."""
           links = []

           for obj in package.get("objectives", []):
               obj_id = obj.get("id")
               if not obj_id:
                   continue

               obj_type = self._get_type_from_id(obj_id)
               consolidated = obj.get("consolidated", False)

               # linked_requirements → primary derivation
               for req_id in obj.get("linked_requirements", []):
                   req_type = self._get_type_from_id(req_id)
                   links.append({
                       "from_step": 2,
                       "from_type": req_type,
                       "from_id": req_id,
                       "to_step": step_number,
                       "to_type": obj_type,
                       "to_id": obj_id,
                       "link_type": "achieves",
                       "consolidated": consolidated,
                   })

               # acceptance_criteria → link back to Step 1
               for crt_id in obj.get("acceptance_criteria", []):
                   links.append({
                       "from_step": 1,
                       "from_type": "success_criterion",
                       "from_id": crt_id,
                       "to_step": step_number,
                       "to_type": obj_type,
                       "to_id": obj_id,
                       "link_type": "traces_to",
                   })

           # trace_map entries (may duplicate linked_requirements, idempotency
  handles this)
           for trace in package.get("trace_map", []):
               obj_id = trace.get("objective_id")
               if not obj_id:
                   continue

               obj_type = self._get_type_from_id(obj_id)
               consolidated = trace.get("consolidated", False)

               for req_id in trace.get("requirement_ids", []):
                   req_type = self._get_type_from_id(req_id)
                   links.append({
                       "from_step": 2,
                       "from_type": req_type,
                       "from_id": req_id,
                       "to_step": step_number,
                       "to_type": obj_type,
                       "to_id": obj_id,
                       "link_type": "achieves",
                       "consolidated": consolidated,
                   })

           return links

       def _get_type_from_id(self, element_id: str) -> str:
           """Infer element type from ID prefix."""
           if not element_id or "-" not in element_id:
               return "unknown"

           prefix = element_id.split("-")[0]
           type_map = {
               "SH": "stakeholder",
               "HC": "hard_constraint",
               "SC": "soft_constraint",
               "IN": "scope_in",
               "OUT": "scope_out",
               "CRT": "success_criterion",
               "ASM": "assumption",
               "OQ": "open_question",
               "IP": "integration_point",
               "FR": "functional_requirement",
               "NFR": "non_functional_requirement",
               "CR": "constraint_requirement",
               "IR": "interface_requirement",
               "CAP": "capability_objective",
               "QUAL": "quality_objective",
               "COMP": "compliance_objective",
               "INTF": "interface_objective",
           }
           return type_map.get(prefix, "unknown")

       async def get_forward_traces(
           self,
           workflow_id: UUID,
           from_id: str,
       ) -> list[TraceabilityLink]:
           """Get all links originating from an element."""
           return await self._trace_repo.list_from_element(workflow_id, from_id)

       async def get_backward_traces(
           self,
           workflow_id: UUID,
           to_id: str,
       ) -> list[TraceabilityLink]:
           """Get all links pointing to an element."""
           return await self._trace_repo.list_to_element(workflow_id, to_id)

       async def list_all_links(
           self,
           workflow_id: UUID,
       ) -> list[TraceabilityLink]:
           """Get all traceability links for a workflow."""
           return await self._trace_repo.list_for_workflow(workflow_id)

       async def verify_coverage(
           self,
           workflow_id: UUID,
       ) -> dict[str, Any]:
           """Verify traceability coverage across steps.

           Returns dict with coverage statistics and any gaps.
           """
           links = await self._trace_repo.list_for_workflow(workflow_id)

           # Group by step transitions
           step1_to_step2 = [l for l in links if l.from_step == 1 and l.to_step
  == 2]
           step2_to_step3 = [l for l in links if l.from_step == 2 and l.to_step
  == 3]
           step1_to_step3 = [l for l in links if l.from_step == 1 and l.to_step
  == 3]

           return {
               "total_links": len(links),
               "step1_to_step2": len(step1_to_step2),
               "step2_to_step3": len(step2_to_step3),
               "step1_to_step3": len(step1_to_step3),
               "has_step1_coverage": len(step1_to_step2) > 0,
               "has_step2_coverage": len(step2_to_step3) > 0,
               "valid": len(step1_to_step2) > 0 and len(step2_to_step3) > 0,
           }

   Phase 2: Workflow Service Integration

   # At top of workflow_service.py, add import:
   from application.traceability_service import TraceabilityService

       # Store package
       artifact = await self._artifact_service.store_step_package(...)
       result[(PassType.EXECUTION, step_key)] = artifact.id

       # P5.4: Extract and persist traceability links

           await self._traceability_service.extract_and_persist_links(
               workflow_id=workflow_db_id,
               step_name=step_name,
               package_content=artifact.content_jsonb,  # Use stored package
  with metadata
           )
           logger.info(f"Extracted traceability links for {step_key}")
       except Exception as e:
           logger.error(f"Failed to extract trace links for {step_key}: {e}")
           # Continue - trace extraction failure shouldn't block artifact
  storage

   Phase 3: API Endpoint (routes/workflows.py)

   Add the traceability endpoint:

   from application.traceability_service import TraceabilityService

   @router.get("/{workflow_id}/traceability")
   async def get_traceability(
       workflow_id: str,
       session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
   ):
       """Get traceability links across steps."""
       # Resolve workflow
       workflow_repo = WorkflowRepository(session)
       workflow = await workflow_repo.get_by_external_id(workflow_id)
       if not workflow:
           raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Workflow not found:
  {workflow_id}")

       # Get all links
       traceability_service = TraceabilityService(session)
       links = await traceability_service.list_all_links(workflow.id)

       # Get coverage verification
       coverage = await traceability_service.verify_coverage(workflow.id)

       return {
           "workflow_id": workflow_id,
           "links": [
               {
                   "id": str(link.id),
                   "from_step": link.from_step,
                   "from_type": link.from_type,
                   "from_id": link.from_id,
                   "to_step": link.to_step,
                   "to_type": link.to_type,
                   "to_id": link.to_id,
                   "link_type": link.link_type,
                   "consolidated": link.consolidated,
               }
               for link in links
           ],
           "coverage": coverage,
       }

   Phase 4: Verification Tool (tools/verify_traces.py)

   #!/usr/bin/env python3
   """
   Gate B Verification: Traceability Links

   Usage: python tools/verify_traces.py --workflow <workflow_id>
   """

   import argparse
   import asyncio
   import sys

   from sqlalchemy import select, func
   from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import create_async_engine, AsyncSession
   from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

   from infrastructure.db.models import Artifact, TraceabilityLink
   from infrastructure.db.models.enums import PassType


   async def verify_traces(workflow_id: str, database_url: str) -> bool:
       """Verify traceability links exist for a workflow."""
       engine = create_async_engine(database_url)
       async_session = sessionmaker(engine, class_=AsyncSession,
  expire_on_commit=False)

       async with async_session() as session:
           # Get workflow UUID from external ID
           from infrastructure.db.models import Workflow
           stmt = select(Workflow).where(Workflow.workflow_id == workflow_id)
           result = await session.execute(stmt)
           workflow = result.scalar_one_or_none()

           if not workflow:
               print(f"ERROR: Workflow not found: {workflow_id}")
               return False

           # Count trace links by step transition
           stmt = (
               select(
                   TraceabilityLink.from_step,
                   TraceabilityLink.to_step,
                   func.count(TraceabilityLink.id).label("count"),
               )
               .where(TraceabilityLink.workflow_id == workflow.id)
               .group_by(TraceabilityLink.from_step, TraceabilityLink.to_step)
           )
           result = await session.execute(stmt)
           counts = {(row.from_step, row.to_step): row.count for row in result}

           print(f"Workflow: {workflow_id}")
           print(f"Step 1 → Step 2 links: {counts.get((1, 2), 0)}")
           print(f"Step 2 → Step 3 links: {counts.get((2, 3), 0)}")
           print(f"Step 1 → Step 3 links: {counts.get((1, 3), 0)}")

           # Gate B requirement: Step 2 and 3 must have incoming traces
           step2_has_traces = counts.get((1, 2), 0) > 0
           step3_has_traces = counts.get((2, 3), 0) > 0

           if step2_has_traces and step3_has_traces:
               print("✓ Gate B: Traceability links valid")
               return True
           else:
               print("✗ Gate B: Missing traceability links")
               if not step2_has_traces:
                   print("  - Step 2 missing traces from Step 1")
               if not step3_has_traces:
                   print("  - Step 3 missing traces from Step 2")
               return False


   def main():
       parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Verify traceability links
  (Gate B)")
       parser.add_argument("--workflow", required=True, help="Workflow ID to
  verify")
       parser.add_argument("--database-url", default=None, help="Database URL
  (or use DATABASE_URL env)")
       args = parser.parse_args()

       import os
       database_url = args.database_url or os.environ.get("DATABASE_URL")
       if not database_url:
           print("ERROR: DATABASE_URL not set")
           sys.exit(1)

       success = asyncio.run(verify_traces(args.workflow, database_url))
       sys.exit(0 if success else 1)


   if __name__ == "__main__":
       main()

   Phase 5: Unit Tests (test_traceability_service.py)

   """Unit tests for TraceabilityService link extraction."""

   import pytest
   from uuid import uuid4

   from application.traceability_service import TraceabilityService


   class TestGetTypeFromId:
       """Test ID prefix to type mapping."""

       def test_stakeholder_id(self):
           service = TraceabilityService.__new__(TraceabilityService)
           assert service._get_type_from_id("SH-001") == "stakeholder"

       def test_functional_requirement_id(self):
           service = TraceabilityService.__new__(TraceabilityService)
           assert service._get_type_from_id("FR-001") ==
  "functional_requirement"

       def test_capability_objective_id(self):
           service = TraceabilityService.__new__(TraceabilityService)
           assert service._get_type_from_id("CAP-001") == "capability_objective"

       def test_unknown_prefix(self):
           service = TraceabilityService.__new__(TraceabilityService)
           assert service._get_type_from_id("XX-001") == "unknown"


   class TestExtractStep2Links:
       """Test Step 1 → Step 2 link extraction."""

       def test_extracts_source_derives_link(self):
           service = TraceabilityService.__new__(TraceabilityService)
           package = {
               "requirements": [{
                   "id": "FR-001",
                   "source": {"type": "stakeholder", "id": "SH-001", "aspect":
  "need"},
                   "trace": {},
               }]
           }
           links = service._extract_step2_links(2, package)

           assert len(links) == 1
           assert links[0]["from_id"] == "SH-001"
           assert links[0]["to_id"] == "FR-001"
           assert links[0]["link_type"] == "derives"

       def test_extracts_trace_stakeholders(self):
           service = TraceabilityService.__new__(TraceabilityService)
           package = {
               "requirements": [{
                   "id": "FR-001",
                   "source": {"type": "stakeholder", "id": "SH-001", "aspect":
  "need"},
                   "trace": {"stakeholders": ["SH-001", "SH-002"]},
               }]
           }
           links = service._extract_step2_links(2, package)

           # 1 derives + 2 traces_to
           assert len(links) == 3
           traces_to = [l for l in links if l["link_type"] == "traces_to"]
           assert len(traces_to) == 2


   class TestExtractStep3Links:
       """Test Step 2 → Step 3 link extraction."""

       def test_extracts_linked_requirements(self):
           service = TraceabilityService.__new__(TraceabilityService)
           package = {
               "objectives": [{
                   "id": "CAP-001",
                   "linked_requirements": ["FR-001", "FR-002"],
                   "consolidated": True,
                   "acceptance_criteria": [],
               }],
               "trace_map": [],
           }
           links = service._extract_step3_links(3, package)

           assert len(links) == 2
           assert all(l["link_type"] == "achieves" for l in links)
           assert all(l["consolidated"] is True for l in links)

       def test_extracts_acceptance_criteria_back_to_step1(self):
           service = TraceabilityService.__new__(TraceabilityService)
           package = {
               "objectives": [{
                   "id": "CAP-001",
                   "linked_requirements": [],
                   "acceptance_criteria": ["CRT-001"],
               }],
               "trace_map": [],
           }
           links = service._extract_step3_links(3, package)

           assert len(links) == 1
           assert links[0]["from_step"] == 1
           assert links[0]["from_id"] == "CRT-001"
           assert links[0]["link_type"] == "traces_to"

   Key Design Decisions

   | Decision                       | Choice
  |
   Rationale                                 |
   
  |--------------------------------|--------------------------------------------
  ---------------------|----
   ---------------------------------------|
   | Extract after artifact storage | Yes
  | Use
    stored package with injected metadata |
   | Idempotent link creation       | Yes
  |
   Safe for resume/retry scenarios           |
   | Continue on extraction failure | Yes
  |
   Trace extraction shouldn't block workflow |
   | Infer type from ID prefix      | Yes
  |
   Simpler than storing type mappings        |
   | Store consolidated flag        | Yes
  |
   Preserve consolidation info from Step 3   |
   | derives vs traces_to           | derives for primary source, traces_to for
  additional references |
   Semantic distinction                      |
   | achieves for Step 3            | Yes
  |
   Objectives "achieve" requirements         |

   Verification Commands

   # Unit tests
   PYTHONPATH=apps/api pytest apps/api/tests/unit/test_traceability_service.py
  -v

   # All unit tests
   PYTHONPATH=apps/api pytest apps/api/tests/unit/ -v

   # Gate B verification (requires completed workflow)
   python tools/verify_traces.py --workflow <workflow_id>

   Scope Boundaries

   In Scope (P5.4):
   - TraceabilityService with link extraction logic
   - Integration into workflow_service.sync_artifacts_from_state()
   - GET /workflows/{id}/traceability endpoint
   - verify_traces.py tool
   - Unit tests for extraction logic

    Key gaps vs directives/spec

  Responding with gemini-3-pro-preview
✦ I have evaluated Co-Developer-1's critique.

  Evaluation:
  Co-Developer-1 makes several critical points about data integrity and scope
  that supersede the Senior Developer's initial approach.

   1. Revision Safety (Replace vs. Insert-Idempotent): Co-Developer-1 is
      absolutely correct. If a user revises Step 2, the old requirements might
      disappear. An insert-only approach leaves "zombie links" pointing to
      deleted requirements. Correction: Use a "Replace for Step" strategy
      (Delete all links where to_step=N and workflow_id=ID, then insert new
      ones).
   2. Source of Truth (Coverage Map): Doc 3 does define coverage_map (Step 2)
      and trace_map (Step 3) as rigorous traceability artifacts. Parsing
      individual requirement objects is brittle. Relying on the maps forces the
      LLM's explicit "Traceability Mapping" phase to be the source of truth.
      Correction: Parse coverage_map and trace_map.
   3. Cross-Step Links: Doc 2 emphasizes the 1->2->3 chain. Linking Step 3 back
      to Step 1 (Acceptance Criteria) might be valid in reality, but it
      complicates the strict chain verification. Correction: Stick to 1->2 and
      2->3 links for now.
   4. Verification Tool: verify_traces.py is indeed P6.2 (Gate B test). Moving
      it there keeps P5.4 focused on extraction logic. Correction: Defer tool.

  Perspective:
  Co-Developer-1's approach is safer (no zombie links) and more aligned with the
  "Map-based" output structure we enforced in P5.2 prompts.

  Revised Consolidated Plan (P5.4 - Final):

  Objective: Implement TraceabilityService to extract links from coverage_map
  (Step 2) and trace_map (Step 3) and persist them using a "Replace-per-Step"
  strategy to ensure sync with revisions.

  Files:
   1. apps/api/application/traceability_service.py (Implementation)
   2. apps/api/application/workflow_service.py (Integration)
   3. apps/api/tests/unit/test_traceability_service.py (Unit tests)

  Logic:
   * Method: update_links_for_step(workflow_id, step_number, package_content)
   * Step 1: Return (no incoming links).
   * Step 2: Parse coverage_map. Create TraceabilityLink (Type: derives) from
     Source -> Requirement.
   * Step 3: Parse trace_map. Create TraceabilityLink (Type: achieves) from
     Requirement -> Objective.
   * Persistence: Transaction: DELETE FROM traceability_links WHERE
     workflow_id=... AND to_step=... -> INSERT ....

  Command: "Begin slice P5.4"

  where code must intentionally violate an authoritative spec"), here are the
  potential candidates:

   1. P5.1 LLM Adapter: Use of `httpx` vs. generic adapter: Doc 3 §10.2
      specifies an ObservabilityAdapter but is vague on the LLM adapter
      interface. Implementing a custom OpenAIResponsesAdapter using raw httpx
      instead of standard LangChain classes (to satisfy the "Responses API
      shape" requirement) is a specific implementation choice. Verdict: Not a
      deviation. It's a specific implementation of the generic requirement.

   2. P5.2 Prompt Structure: "Content-Only" Output: Doc 3 schemas include
      metadata (package_id, etc.). We chose to have the LLM output only the
      content fields and inject metadata in the service layer. Verdict: Not a
      deviation. This is a standard architectural pattern (DTO vs Domain Model).
      The stored artifact does conform to the schema.

   3. P5.3 Persistence Timing: Doc 2 implies "Pass 2 execution... then
      interrupts". We explicitly chose to persist Pass 1 docs before the gate to
      enable review. Verdict: Clarification/Refinement. This honors the intent
      (reviewable artifacts) better than strict adherence to a simplified flow
      diagram.

   4. P5.4 Traceability Source: We chose coverage_map / trace_map as the
      exclusive source of truth for links, ignoring inline source fields in
      requirements objects if they conflict. Verdict: Clarification. Doc 3
      mentions both; prioritizing the map is a design choice for consistency.

  Conclusion:
  There are no new deviations that rise to the level of "Violating the Authority
  Documents" in a way that requires a DECISIONS.md entry. Most "deviations" were
  actually Spec Corrections (which we handled by updating the spec directly,
  e.g., the SSE event names and paths) or Implementation Details (like the httpx
  adapter) that fit within the architectural boundaries.

  rather than accumulating debt.
